drm\+\_\+eld\+\_\+calc\+\_\+baseline\+\_\+block\+\_\+size -\/ Calculate baseline block size in bytes @eld\+: pointer to an eld memory structure with mnl and sad\+\_\+count set

This is a helper for determining the payload size of the baseline block, in bytes, for e.\+g. setting the Baseline\+\_\+\+E\+L\+D\+\_\+\+Len field in the E\+LD header block. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 2

drm\+\_\+eld\+\_\+size -\/ Get E\+LD size in bytes @eld\+: pointer to a complete eld memory structure

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned value does not include the vendor block. It\textquotesingle{}s vendor specific, and comprises of the remaining bytes in the E\+LD memory buffer after \mbox{\hyperlink{drm__edid_8h_a85481cf7716032bc1df405f0a190d696}{drm\+\_\+eld\+\_\+size()}} bytes of header and baseline block.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned value is guaranteed to be a multiple of 4. \mbox{\Hypertarget{drm__edid_8h_ae207f0639ce2ea36b7321cef0744c9c2}\label{drm__edid_8h_ae207f0639ce2ea36b7321cef0744c9c2}}
